function Add-CT($so){
  $sql = @'
DO $$
DECLARE v_ct bigint;
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO CHUNG_TU (SoCT, NgayLap, NgayGhiSo, MaLoaiCT, MaKy, LyDo)
  VALUES ('@SO@', '2026-03-20', '2026-03-20', 'PKT', '2026-M03', 'Du lieu lab5 lan2')
  RETURNING MaCT INTO v_ct;
  INSERT INTO CHI_TIET_CHUNG_TU (MaCT, SttDong, MaTKNo, MaTKCo, SoTien)
  VALUES (v_ct, 1, '1111', '411', 100000);
  CALL sp_post_voucher(v_ct);
END $$;
'@ -replace '@SO@',$so
  $sql | & $psql @P -p 55491 -d $db -X -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -f -
}

PS C:\Users\Admin> function Restore-PITR($ten,$port,$dongDich){
  $dir = "$lab\restore_$ten"
  if (Test-Path $dir) { Remove-Item $dir -Recurse -Force }
  $tCopy = Measure-Command { robocopy "$lab\backup\base_combined" $dir /E /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null }
  $cfg = @"

port = $port
archive_mode = off
restore_command = 'copy "$arch\\%f" "%p"'
$dongDich
recovery_target_action = 'promote'
"@
  Add-Content "$dir\postgresql.conf" -Value $cfg -Encoding ASCII
  Copy-Item "$dir\postgresql.conf" "$KQ\postgresql_ban_restore_$ten.conf"
  New-Item "$dir\recovery.signal" -ItemType File | Out-Null
  $tRec = Measure-Command {
    & "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -D $dir -l "$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log" start | Out-Null
    $i = 0
    do { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200; $i++
         $r = & $psql @P -p $port -d postgres -X -At -c "SELECT pg_is_in_recovery()" 2>$null
    } until ($r -eq 'f' -or $i -gt 600)
  }
  $ct   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM chung_tu"
  $sc   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM so_cai"
  $lech = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM v_chung_tu_lech"
  $R["PITR ${ten}: sao chep (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: replay+promote (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: chung_tu / so_cai / lech"] = "$ct / $sc / $lech"
  & "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -D $dir stop -m fast | Out-Null
  cls
  "== PITR $ten (port $port) =="
  "Sao chep: {0} s | Replay+promote: {1} s" -f [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1),[math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  "Sau phuc hoi: chung_tu=$ct, so_cai=$sc, lech=$lech"
  "Ky vong     : chung_tu=$ct_truoc, so_cai=$sc_truoc, lech=0"
  Select-String -Path "$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log" -Pattern "recovery stopping|starting point-in-time|selected new timeline" | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
}

PS C:\Users\Admin> function Restore-PITR($ten,$port,$dongDich){
  $dir = "$lab\restore_$ten"
  if (Test-Path $dir) { Remove-Item $dir -Recurse -Force }
  $tCopy = Measure-Command { robocopy "$lab\backup\base_combined" $dir /E /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null }
  $cfg = @"

port = $port
archive_mode = off
restore_command = 'copy "$arch\\%f" "%p"'
$dongDich
recovery_target_action = 'promote'
"@
  Add-Content "$dir\postgresql.conf" -Value $cfg -Encoding ASCII
  Copy-Item "$dir\postgresql.conf" "$KQ\postgresql_ban_restore_$ten.conf"
  New-Item "$dir\recovery.signal" -ItemType File | Out-Null
  $tRec = Measure-Command {
    & "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -D $dir -l "$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log" start
    $i = 0
    do { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200; $i++
         $r = & $psql @P -p $port -d postgres -X -At -c "SELECT pg_is_in_recovery()" 2>$null
    } until ($r -eq 'f' -or $i -gt 600)
  }
  $ct   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM chung_tu"
  $sc   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM so_cai"
  $lech = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM v_chung_tu_lech"
  $R["PITR ${ten}: sao chep (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: replay+promote (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: chung_tu / so_cai / lech"] = "$ct / $sc / $lech"
  & "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -D $dir stop -m fast | Out-Null
  cls
  "== PITR $ten (port $port) =="
  "Sao chep: {0} s | Replay+promote: {1} s" -f [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1),[math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  "Sau phuc hoi: chung_tu=$ct, so_cai=$sc, lech=$lech"
  "Ky vong     : chung_tu=$ct_truoc, so_cai=$sc_truoc, lech=0"
  Select-String -Path "$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log" -Pattern "recovery stopping|starting point-in-time|selected new timeline" | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
}

PS C:\Users\Admin> function Restore-PITR($ten,$port,$dongDich){
  $dir = "$lab\restore_$ten"
  if (Test-Path $dir) { Remove-Item $dir -Recurse -Force }
  $tCopy = Measure-Command { robocopy "$lab\backup\base_combined" $dir /E /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null }
  $cfg = @"

port = $port
archive_mode = off
restore_command = 'copy "$arch\\%f" "%p"'
$dongDich
recovery_target_action = 'promote'
"@
  Add-Content "$dir\postgresql.conf" -Value $cfg -Encoding ASCII
  Copy-Item "$dir\postgresql.conf" "$KQ\postgresql_ban_restore_$ten.conf"
  New-Item "$dir\recovery.signal" -ItemType File | Out-Null
  $tRec = Measure-Command {
    Start-Process -FilePath "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -ArgumentList "-D `"$dir`" -l `"$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log`" start" -WindowStyle Hidden
    $i = 0
    do { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 200; $i++
         $r = & $psql @P -p $port -d postgres -X -At -c "SELECT pg_is_in_recovery()" 2>$null
    } until ($r -eq 'f' -or $i -gt 600)
  }
  $ct   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM chung_tu"
  $sc   = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM so_cai"
  $lech = Q $port $db "SELECT count(*) FROM v_chung_tu_lech"
  $R["PITR ${ten}: sao chep (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: replay+promote (giay)"] = [math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  $R["PITR ${ten}: chung_tu / so_cai / lech"] = "$ct / $sc / $lech"
  & "$bin\pg_ctl.exe" -D $dir stop -m fast | Out-Null
  cls
  "== PITR $ten (port $port) =="
  "Sao chep: {0} s | Replay+promote: {1} s" -f [math]::Round($tCopy.TotalSeconds,1),[math]::Round($tRec.TotalSeconds,1)
  "Sau phuc hoi: chung_tu=$ct, so_cai=$sc, lech=$lech"
  "Ky vong     : chung_tu=$ct_truoc, so_cai=$sc_truoc, lech=0"
  Select-String -Path "$KQ\pitr_${ten}_restore.log" -Pattern "recovery stopping|starting point-in-time|selected new timeline" | ForEach-Object { $_.Line }
}

PS C:\Users\Admin> $bin = "C:\Program Files\PostgreSQL\18\bin"
$P = @("-h","127.0.0.1","-U","postgres")

& "$bin\psql.exe" @P -p 55491 -d ketoan -c "
SELECT ct.soct, ct.mact, COUNT(sc.*) AS so_dong_so_cai
FROM chung_tu ct
LEFT JOIN so_cai sc ON sc.mact = ct.mact
WHERE ct.soct LIKE 'L2-RPO%'
GROUP BY ct.soct, ct.mact
ORDER BY ct.soct;
"

PS C:\Users\Admin> $rpo = foreach ($k in 1..3) {
  Add-CT "L2-RPO$k" | Out-Null
  $ts = Q 55491 $db "SELECT to_char(clock_timestamp(),'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.USOF')"
  $n = 0
  do { Start-Sleep 1; $n++
       $d = Q 55491 postgres "SELECT round(extract(epoch FROM (last_archived_time - '$ts'::timestamptz))::numeric,1) FROM pg_stat_archiver"
  } until (($d -and [double]$d -gt 0) -or $n -gt 150)
  [double]$d
}

cls
